# Actions, resources, and condition keys for Amazon Connect

Amazon Connect (service prefix: `connect`) provides the following
service-specific operations, resources, actions, and condition keys for use in IAM permission
policies.

References:

- Learn how to [configure this service](../../../connect/latest/adminguide.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide.md").
- View a list of the [API operations available for
  this service](../../../connect/latest/APIReference.md "../../../connect/latest/APIReference.md").
- Learn how to secure this service and its resources by
  [using IAM](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security-iam.md") permission policies.
- View the [programmatic service authorization
  reference](https://servicereference.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/v1/connect/connect.json "https://servicereference.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/v1/connect/connect.json") for this service.

###### Topics

- [API operations defined by Amazon Connect](#list_connect-operations "#list_connect-operations")
- [Actions defined by Amazon Connect](#list_connect-actions-as-permissions "#list_connect-actions-as-permissions")
- [Permission-only actions for Amazon Connect](#list_connect-permission-only-actions "#list_connect-permission-only-actions")
- [Resource types defined by Amazon Connect](#list_connect-resources-for-iam-policies "#list_connect-resources-for-iam-policies")
- [Condition keys for Amazon Connect](#list_connect-policy-keys "#list_connect-policy-keys")

## API operations defined by Amazon Connect

The following table maps API operations to the IAM actions they authorize. Only condition keys that have static values for the given API and action are listed; for the full set of condition keys supported by each action, see the [Actions table](#list_connect-actions-as-permissions "#list_connect-actions-as-permissions").

## Actions defined by Amazon Connect

You can specify the following actions in the `Action` element of an IAM
policy statement. Use policies to grant permissions to perform an operation in AWS. When
you use an action in a policy, you usually allow or deny access to the API operation or CLI
command with the same name. However, in some cases, a single action controls access to more
than one operation. Alternatively, some operations require several different actions.

| Actions                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Resource types (\*required)                                                                                                                    | Condition keys                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Access level   |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------- |
| [ActivateEvaluationForm](../../../connect/latest/APIReference/API_ActivateEvaluationForm.md "../../../connect/latest/APIReference/API_ActivateEvaluationForm.md")                                                             | Grants permission to activate an evaluation form in the specified Amazon Connect instance. After the evaluation form is activated, it is available to start new evaluations based on the form                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | [evaluation-form\*](#list_connect-resource-evaluation-form "#list_connect-resource-evaluation-form")                                           | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")<br>[connect:InstanceId](#list_connect-connect_InstanceId "#list_connect-connect_InstanceId")                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Write          |

## Permission-only actions for Amazon Connect

The following actions are defined by Amazon Connect but are not directly
invocable through any API operation. They can only be used in IAM policy statements
to grant or deny permissions.

| Actions                                                                                                                                                                                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Resource types (\*required)                                                     | Condition keys                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Access level |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ |
| [AssociateCustomerProfilesDomain](../../../console/connect/amazon-connect-console/grant-instance-permissions.md "../../../console/connect/amazon-connect-console/grant-instance-permissions.md")    | Grants permission to associate a Customer Profiles domain for an existing Amazon Connect instance                                                                                                                                                                                               | [instance\*](#list_connect-resource-instance "#list_connect-resource-instance") | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")                                                                                                                                                                                  | Write        |
| [DescribeForecastingPlanningSchedulingIntegration](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/optimization-apis.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/optimization-apis.md")                               | Grants permission to describe the status of forecasting, planning, and scheduling integration on an Amazon Connect instance                                                                                                                                                                     | [instance\*](#list_connect-resource-instance "#list_connect-resource-instance") | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")<br>[connect:InstanceId](#list_connect-connect_InstanceId "#list_connect-connect_InstanceId")                                                                                     | Read         |
| [DisassociateCustomerProfilesDomain](../../../console/connect/amazon-connect-console/grant-instance-permissions.md "../../../console/connect/amazon-connect-console/grant-instance-permissions.md") | Grants permission to disassociate a Customer Profiles domain for an existing Amazon Connect instance                                                                                                                                                                                            | [instance\*](#list_connect-resource-instance "#list_connect-resource-instance") | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")                                                                                                                                                                                  | Write        |
| [SendIntegrationEvent](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/whatsapp-integration.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/whatsapp-integration.md")                                                     | Grants permission to send integration events using the Amazon Connect API                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |                                                                                 |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Write        |
| [SendOutboundChatMessage](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/enable-outbound-campaigns.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/enable-outbound-campaigns.md")                                        | Grants permission to send outbound chat messages using the Amazon Connect API                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | [instance\*](#list_connect-resource-instance "#list_connect-resource-instance") | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")<br>[connect:InstanceId](#list_connect-connect_InstanceId "#list_connect-connect_InstanceId")<br>[connect:Subtype](#list_connect-connect_Subtype "#list_connect-connect_Subtype") | Write        |
| [phone-number\*](#list_connect-resource-phone-number "#list_connect-resource-phone-number")                                                                                                         | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")<br>[connect:InstanceId](#list_connect-connect_InstanceId "#list_connect-connect_InstanceId")<br>[connect:Subtype](#list_connect-connect_Subtype "#list_connect-connect_Subtype") |
| [SendOutboundWebNotification](../../../connect/latest/APIReference/API_SendOutboundWebNotification.md "../../../connect/latest/APIReference/API_SendOutboundWebNotification.md")                    | Grants permission to send outbound web notifications using the Amazon Connect API                                                                                                                                                                                                               | [instance\*](#list_connect-resource-instance "#list_connect-resource-instance") | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")<br>[connect:InstanceId](#list_connect-connect_InstanceId "#list_connect-connect_InstanceId")                                                                                     | Write        |
| [StartForecastingPlanningSchedulingIntegration](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/optimization-apis.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/optimization-apis.md")                                  | Grants permission to enable forecasting, planning, and scheduling integration on an Amazon Connect instance                                                                                                                                                                                     | [instance\*](#list_connect-resource-instance "#list_connect-resource-instance") | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_")<br>[connect:InstanceId](#list_connect-connect_InstanceId "#list_connect-connect_InstanceId")                                                                                     | Write        |

`Resource` element of IAM permission policy statements.

| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [agent-status](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/metrics-agent-status.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/metrics-agent-status.md")                                         | arn:${Partition}:connect:${Region}:${Account}:instance/${InstanceId}/agent-state/${AgentStatusId}                        | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") |
| [ai-agent](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/create-ai-agents.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/create-ai-agents.md")                                                     | arn:${Partition}:wisdom:${Region}:${Account}:ai-agent/${AssistantId}/${AIAgentId}:${Version}                             | [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_ "#list_connect-aws_ResourceTag___TagKey_") |

## Condition keys for Amazon Connect

Amazon Connect defines the following condition keys that can be used in the
`Condition` element of an IAM policy.

| Condition keys                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Description                                                                                                                    | Type          |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------- |
| [aws:RequestTag/${TagKey}](../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-requesttag "../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-requesttag")    | Filters access by using tag key-value pairs in the request                                                                     | String        |
| [aws:ResourceTag/${TagKey}](../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-resourcetag "../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-resourcetag") | Filters access by using tag key-value pairs attached to the resource                                                           | String        |
| [aws:TagKeys](../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-tagkeys "../../../IAM/latest/UserGuide/reference_policies_condition-keys.md#condition-keys-tagkeys")                       | Filters access by using tag keys in the request                                                                                | ArrayOfString |
| [connect:AssignmentType](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                        | Filters access by restricting access to create contacts based on Assignment Type                                               | String        |
| [connect:AttributeType](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                         | Filters access by the attribute type of the Amazon Connect instance                                                            | String        |
| [connect:Channel](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                               | Filters access by Channel                                                                                                      | String        |
| [connect:ContactAssociationId](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                  | Filters access by ContactAssociationId                                                                                         | String        |
| [connect:ContactInitiationMethod](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                               | Filters access by restricting access to create contacts based on the initiation method of the contact                          | String        |
| [connect:ExpressionValue](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                       | Filters access by restricting data table operations based on expression type                                                   | String        |
| [connect:FlowType](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                              | Filters access by Flow type                                                                                                    | ArrayOfString |
| [connect:InstanceId](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                            | Filters access by restricting federation into specified Amazon Connect instances                                               | String        |
| [connect:ListRealtimeContactAnalysisSegmentsByOutputType](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                       | Filters access by restricting the listed segments using the output type of the Amazon Connect Contact Lens real-time segment   | String        |
| [connect:ListRealtimeContactAnalysisSegmentsBySegmentType](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                      | Filters access by restricting the listed segments using the segment types of the Amazon Connect Contact Lens real-time segment | ArrayOfString |
| [connect:MonitorCapabilities](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                   | Filters access by restricting the monitor capabilities of the user in the request                                              | ArrayOfString |
| [connect:PreferredUserArn](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                      | Filters access by PreferredUserArn                                                                                             | ARN           |
| [connect:PrimaryAttribute/${PrimaryAttribute}](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                  | Filters access by restricting which primary attributes the user can manage                                                     | String        |
| [connect:SearchContactsByContactAnalysis](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                       | Filters access by restricting searches using analysis outputs from Amazon Connect Contact Lens                                 | ArrayOfString |
| [connect:SearchTag/${TagKey}](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                   | Filters access by TagFilter condition passed in the search request                                                             | String        |
| [connect:StorageResourceType](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                   | Filters access by restricting the storage resource type of the Amazon Connect instance storage configuration                   | String        |
| [connect:Subtype](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                               | Filters access by restricting creation of a contact for specific subtypes                                                      | String        |
| [connect:UserArn](../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md "../../../connect/latest/adminguide/security_iam_service-with-iam.md")                                                               | Filters access by UserArn                                                                                                      | ARN           |
